# 0018. Proofs of all app decisions

**Status:** in progress, 2026-11-07. **Rulings:** VL-9, VL-29. Lane `vl-proveall`.

The proof consumes production VibeLang modules in LemmaScript's plain TypeScript subset, with unchanged `//@`
contracts. Effect shells call those same decisions. The harness discovers every marked module under `src/`,
`worker/` and `scripts/`; omission of an emitted marked function fails. Mutations change executable text only,
strictly typecheck, and must fail a Dafny obligation in their named function. Tool errors never count as rejection.

## Proofs and boundaries

| Production module | What is proved | Boundary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `src/history/mythical.vibe` | Numbered history events, identity/rank uniqueness, epic membership, preservation of epics, real-commit prefixes and every commit's notes (a prefix, at most one appended), exactly-one folds, and a trailer folding only into an approved or landed commit (`foldTarget`) (0020) | Sequencer locking, store-before-publish, the trailer regexp and the bootstrap's grouping are tested, not proved |
| `src/history/flowMachine.vibe` | Who may take each history action; authorize/record ordering; shared role thresholds, HTTP status/outcome mapping, Plan validation/field initialization, open-epic membership/append prefix, signatures and labels (0020) | Engine execution and journal replay, `Caller`'s source (0019), full page/model transitions and native codecs |
| `src/core/sandboxDecision.vibe` | Role admission, running count, elapsed usage, nonnegative remaining allowance, ledger uniqueness, sequence and clock high-water preservation, and lease bounds (0024) | Host normalization, external containers and durable scheduling remain boundaries; exact guard and frame mutation receipts are recorded below |
| `src/core/mockDecision.vibe` | Keyword membership, first matching script and fallback only if none matches | Input is already normalized; Unicode casing and script constants are outside the proof |
| `src/core/protocolDecision.vibe` | Conversation/body length bounds; nonempty CLI/MCP identifiers and nonnegative cursors; exact method/subroute mapping; HTTP refusal/success; mock/live choice | Integer schema and URI/JSON/Schema decoding remain runtime boundaries; schema annotations are preserved |
| `src/web/theatreDecision.vibe` | Target selectors, speech priority and redraw identity, bounded outcome labels and focus wrapping | Speech/DOM/storage execution, persisted JSON shape and layout geometry |
| `src/web/modelDecision.vibe` | Update decisions: account failure text and retry routing, sign-in and role gates, people pane sizing, account-change and grant detection, stale-response fences for actions, issues and wiki, monotone actions clock, bounded slash highlight, sandbox minute notices and the running transition | The arms of `update` that spread these results into the Model, command dispatch, and the arrow-key delta of plus or minus one supplied by `view.vibe` |
| `src/web/startupDecision.vibe` | Explicit query/default precedence, preference writes, authoritative role-list selection, saved tutorial controls and opposite-mode selection | URL/storage effects, denial fallback and Foldkit execution |
| `src/web/screenDecision.vibe` | Escape priority, palette focus choice, minimizing eligibility, docs toggle, scroll revision/pausing/following, tour stream cursor, completion announcements and keyboard decisions | Dafny models numbers as integers. Fractional DOM coordinates, IEEE-754 arithmetic, native DOM effects and actual focus restoration are tested, not proved |
| `src/core/conversation.vibe` | Conversation sequence, entry identity and correlation, at most one answer per post, terminal replies/tours ignore later text, exact entry/state/role frames, admissible intent completeness, stable pending removal, unanswered order, replay bounds, reply/tour guards and pane ownership | Integer sequences below MAX_SAFE_INTEGER; dense readonly arrays; UTF-16 strings |
| `src/core/conversation.vibe` issue helpers (0022) | Exact numbered observations, operation-to-role policy, commitment/review field preservation and remaining actions after partial review; bounded first-match ancestor path reaches the root without the child; VIP author and non-removed document admission | Global graph preservation and acceptance completeness remain unproved; host cursor capacity and review-call preconditions remain to audit; GitHub execution is an Effect boundary |
| `worker/liveDispatchDecision.vibe` | Exact scan/attempt/done transitions, first-unmarked cursor, terminal idempotency and one engine start attempt per invocation | Storage-mark truth, engine idempotency and host serialization |
| `worker/replyProgressDecision.vibe` | Complete event-kind dispatch and ignored/non-text identity, closed safe nonnegative token invariant, zero base/exact settled sum, validation admission, text/retry/failure frames, invalidity latch and completion precedence | Native safe-integer observations establish the domain; SDK payload/DoneText decoding and durable replay remain outside these contracts |
| `worker/replyReasons.vibe` | Content policy → refused; credit and quota → credit; other model failures → unavailable. Agent precedence: unresolved seat, structured-output failure, model failure, generic failure | The observed tag and code are inputs; constant wording lookup and provider parsing are outside this decision |
| `worker/budgetDecision.vibe` | Strict admission, repeated charges add zero, exact nonnegative charge/total relation, admission to the safe integer sum domain, and exact charge-key construction namespaced by conversation object | Runtime guards establish the integer domain; key uniqueness is concat-cancellation, beyond the backend's string reasoning (`string-concat-unique.ts`) and demonstrated by the two-conversation workerd regression; storage consistency, serialization and host rounding remain boundaries |
| `src/surfaces/commandDecision.vibe` | Own-post/reply correlation, exact event-number prefix/order, delta/restart/failure/finish accumulation, stream termination, outcome and error mapping | Effect stream execution, UUID generation, schema decoding and display formatting |
| `src/core/sequenceDecision.vibe` | First matching post prefix, every eligible source position exactly once in order, source role/text projection, exclusion of unfinished/failed/tour answers, and latest-turn window | The model call, storage and provider tokenization |
| `src/core/flowDecision.vibe` | Post/StartTour/EndTour record-before-follow-up and Reply start/composition/settlement order; Tour active/ready/one-speaker/terminal order with exact closed at-most-one counters; only accepted record dispatches; only matching attempt/offset appends; first undispatched source index; only the addressed terminal event ends a tour; repeated starts remain done; exact reading hold with a four-second floor and 200-word-per-minute rate | Durable engine journaling, scheduling, cancellation, storage and eventual completion are not proved |

A proof of these decisions does not prove Effect, Foldkit, HTTP, JSON, the compiler, Dafny/Z3, or Cloudflare.
Unproved logic remains explicitly visible below; a passing gate must never be reported as all functionality proved.

## Gate receipts, first module batch

Baseline: 33 Dafny obligations and 65/65 rejected mutations. This batch: 44 obligations across four production
modules and 84 executable mutations (19 new). The proof gate runs three harness regressions and four inventory
regressions; the inventory gate rejects a stale table. Both proof and mutation targets participate in ordinary CI.
Typechecking passes; 177 unit/integration tests and 13 local workerd tests pass (two opt-in live-model cases skipped).
After rebasing the mythical-history batch, the shared gate proves 63 obligations in five modules and the full suite
passes 203 tests. The original four proof sources and all 84 mutation inputs are unchanged from their green rerun.
The host report records receipts, exits and subsequent batch counts.

## Second module batch

The production gate proves 99 obligations across eight modules. The unchanged implementation of the conversation
machine carries stronger contracts; browser/protocol/mock shells call extracted policies. The unit suite remains
224/224 after the history-page and post/tour-ordering rebases. The browser suite passes 90 local cases after the history-page rebase (six live/deployment cases skipped). The full mutation gate rejects 163/163 executable mutations (79 new), all with unchanged contracts; the full run took 1,075.616 seconds with four workers on the shared host. After the next rebase, all 11 inherited history mutations also reject: 174/174 across the two invocations. Regenerating the full 174-case input set produces byte-identical copies and manifest entries for those receipts.

A mutation-generator defect counted identical text inside the next function's annotations as executable text.
The generator now excludes matches starting in contract lines and preserves multiline assertion anchors. Two
regressions join the harness; every mutation still preserves all contracts byte for byte. Large negative cases name
a specific production postcondition, selected within their function with fail-closed regression tests. Dafny checks
that postcondition in isolation; the positive gate still checks every obligation. Solver timeouts never count as
rejections. The harness now runs seven regressions. Positive batches have a 60-second solver budget; negative cases retain 30 seconds. No timeout counts as a proof or rejection.

## Third module batch: history and replay boundaries

The history machine now specifies every apply transition's full result and unchanged fields, exact first-match
lookups, the endOf helper's append rank, and refusal precedence. The accepted Plan decision's correspondence to
that append/insertion rank is still pending; its current contract establishes the bound, not that exact value. It proves `0 <= rank < seq` inductive through both decide and apply. The writer
uses a proved capacity guard before incrementing the cursor. Runtime schemas admit only safe integer sequences and
ranks, and ranks below their cursor; storage replay, follow rereads and bootstrap validate these boundaries. This
retains all existing mathematical contracts rather than weakening their domains to hide the JavaScript mismatch.

The client receiver additionally proves next-event advancement and gap closure, unchanged state/pending posts for
ahead-of-cursor events, and exact pending membership after acknowledging a post. At this batch, stable pending order/multiplicity and complete receiver fields were still unproved;
the fifth batch below adds explicit position witnesses and lifts every apply field relation to receive.
`annotation-lambda.ts` retains the exact ordered-filter specification and fails parsing; `method-spec.ts` retains
a verified loop-backed callee in a caller postcondition and fails Dafny expression resolution. Host reproductions
are `~/vl/vibelang-bugs/lemmascript-annotation-lambda/` and `~/vl/vibelang-bugs/lemmascript-method-spec/`.

`verify/bugs/unsafe-integer.ts` and `~/vl/vibelang-bugs/lemmascript-unsafe-integer/` reproduce the integer-model
boundary: native `2 ** 53 + 1` equals `2 ** 53`. The history regression suite fails before the boundary fixes and
passes afterward. After merging the current lanes, typechecking, 352 unit/integration tests, 19 local workerd tests (two opt-in skips), and 123 browser tests (six opt-in skips) pass. Exact mutation counts and exits are in the lane report.
The positive gate passes 117 obligations in eight modules. Isolating generated-source TypeScript projects
retains all inherited compiler options and produces byte-identical Dafny for all eight modules; all 117 obligations
pass in both configurations. Generation no longer reloads unrelated app/test files for each mutant. The current
proof modules are import-free; imported dependency and compiler-configuration drift are not yet covered by the
selected-source hash guard. After the history-flow rebase, the full gate passes 132 obligations in nine modules, including the incoming flow machine and reading-time policy. Mutation negatives now select 60 named production
postconditions, with whole-function checks retained where an earlier assertion is the first failure.
A source-argument regression also showed that extra CLI paths were silently ignored; the checker now validates
every requested path, detects late source edits/additions, and passes ten harness regressions. The inventory also distinguishes membership/numeric predicates from wiring,
and native DOM work from pure logic; a failing classification regression now passes with all five inventory tests. The capacity mutants expose the callee's safe-sequence preconditions; the
mutation gate now recognizes these actual Dafny contract failures, while rejecting successful checks, unrelated
functions, resolution errors and timeouts. A regression failed before this recognition was added.

After the sandbox rebase, the full production gate passes **161 obligations in ten modules** in 151.410 seconds.
The full 299-case mutation run passes; regenerating those inputs after rebase yields byte-identical sources and
manifest entries. The three added sandbox cases also fail their intended contracts: **302/302 rejected** across
these identical-input receipts. Typecheck, 411 unit tests, 22 local worker tests (four opt-in skips), and 132 browser tests (six opt-in skips) pass.
The inventory contains 1,303 functions, callbacks and effect blocks; unproved rows remain explicit.
The sandbox lane's exact integer division reproduction is retained at
`~/vl/vibelang-bugs/lemmascript-exact-integer-division/` (0024); integer rounding and host number normalization are
not silently included in the mathematical integer proof.

Additional tool probes distinguish supported operations from gaps. Guarded Map-count invariants, Set membership,
and comparator sort ordering all verify; these operations are not excuses for leaving selection logic unproved.
`date-day.ts` cannot lower `new Date`, and `nullish-contract.ts` reproduces unsupported `??` annotation syntax.
The latter has equivalent guarded specifications; no contract was weakened. Host fixtures are under
`~/vl/vibelang-bugs/lemmascript-date-day/` and `~/vl/vibelang-bugs/lemmascript-nullish-contract/`.

The rebase also exposed `record-literal-union.ts`: a literal union value inside a returned record
emits a string where Dafny requires the union datatype. The gate rejects this tool failure. Giving the same
wrong-reason mutation a typed local lets it reach the unchanged reason-preservation contract, which rejects it.
`sort-membership.ts` retains length, ordering and both exact membership clauses; the latter fail despite the
backend's permutation axiom. `record-identity.ts` demonstrates the backend's documented structural/reference
identity difference. Their host reproductions are under `~/vl/vibelang-bugs/lemmascript-<fixture-name>/`.
None of these failures is counted as a mutation rejection or a successful proof.

The remaining applicability work after the fifth batch includes the live storage dispatcher order, replay of a
maximum-capacity history snapshot with stale events, safe UI epoch/scroll-intent exhaustion and fractional DOM
coordinates. Conversation frames, pending order, mock FIFO selection, retained-log replay bounds and stored
conversation decoding are covered by that batch as scoped below. History-flow phase proofs do not establish
journal replay idempotency, main-commit traversal order or arbitrary execution interleavings.

## Fourth module batch: commands, context and accounting

The CLI and MCP now call the same proved reply accumulator and error mapper. The live sequencer's model context
uses the proved source-index selection, including the existing exclusion of tour narration; Reply uses the proved
latest-turn window. The index witnesses establish order and multiplicity, including repeated text values.

Budget admission and charging reject corrupt totals and unsafe new sums before writing. A repeated reply ignores
oversized telemetry and preserves the ledger; the final safe integer charge remains admissible. Three regressions
fail before the guards and pass afterward. The existing normalization of nonfinite and negative telemetry remains.

The full gate passes **182 obligations in twelve production modules** (100.802 seconds). All **346 executable
mutations** reject: 44 new cases, four regenerated budget cases, and 298 unchanged inherited inputs. Every current
source and manifest entry is byte-identical to its actual Dafny failure receipt. Typecheck and **416 unit tests**
pass, including shared CLI/MCP and model-history regressions. Local workerd passes 22 tests (four opt-in skips).
The inventory contains 1,317 executable items.

## Fifth module batch: exact conversation and ordered pending posts

`apply` specifies every existing entry field and every appended Posted, ReplyStarted and TourStarted field, plus
exact pane, agent and reply-owner transitions. `receive` carries those same field relations, its exact cursor/gap
behavior, and the new role ledger's exact head, length and preserved older-grant order. Incoming role-grant and
action-authorization policies from 0019 remain in the same machine and proof.

Pending acknowledgement uses an indexed loop with a recursive count of kept source positions. The proof relates
every surviving original position to its exact output position, preserving order and duplicate multiplicity, and
proves the exact output length. `pendingCount` is specification-only; its generated empty companion lemma receives
one explicit recursive proof step, checked afresh by Dafny. All generated contracts and executable bodies remain
unchanged, no axiom or assumption is added, and the harness rejects an unexpected signature or nonempty body.

Unanswered posts are selected by exact eligible source indexes in increasing order; their projection preserves
every selected ID in source order and multiplicity. The mock dispatcher selects the first ID absent from its dispatched set. The live storage
registry remains an Effect boundary; no proof of its interleavings or durable journal is claimed. Replay from
cursor zero proves `0 <= seq <= events.length`, with exact equality for contiguous event numbers. Its bound is
within the native array-length domain, below the safe integer limit. The worker now validates every stored
conversation event before replay, failing with a redacted error before any write or dispatch.

The pre-role-rebase positive receipt proves all twelve modules: 4,386 isolated conversation assertion batches and
150 ordinary obligations in the other eleven modules. Splitting changes the unit of counting, so these are not
summed or compared numerically with earlier unsplit conversation counts. The positive gate checks every assertion;
selected negative cases check the earliest relevant production postcondition or switch-case assertion. The harness
also rejects union-destructor errors mixed with ordinary contract failures; a failing-first regression covers it.
The merged role-policy gate passes all twelve modules: **5,420 isolated conversation assertion batches plus
150 ordinary obligations** in 393.293 seconds. After the issue-helper rebase and two derived receiver-frame
guarantees, the full positive gate passes **5,588 isolated conversation batches plus 150 ordinary obligations** in
235.479 seconds. All inherited contracts remain; issue acyclicity is explicitly still outside the proof.
All **435 current executable mutations** reject: 171 freshly checked conversation mutations, including the incoming
issue cases, and 264 byte-identical inherited sources/manifest entries matched to genuine Dafny failures. A previous
full 424-case run had seven receiver timeouts; a targeted follow-up also exposed one incorrectly selected assertion.
Those failed runs are retained as failures. The corrected targets select the first affected clause or assertion;
all original contracts remain in the positive check. The harness passes fourteen regressions; the installer adds
four download/cache regressions. The inventory contains **1,380 executable items** and remains checked by five
inventory regressions.

After the final rebase, all twelve positive source hashes and all 435 mutation inputs still match their proof
receipts. Typechecking and **453 unit/integration tests** pass after the final malformed-input rebase.
The full media-base browser suite passes **168 tests** (nine opt-in skips); all **45 affected history, recovery and
startup browser cases** pass after that later rebase. Local workerd also passes **23 tests** (four opt-in skips)
on that final malformed-input base. No dependency
was added by this batch. The CI mutation target allows 120 minutes for the complete growing suite with its
conservative two-worker default; every per-obligation limit and failure rule is unchanged.

`verify/bugs/readonly-set.ts` and `~/vl/vibelang-bugs/lemmascript-readonly-set/` reproduce unsupported ReadonlySet
lowering; the supported Set membership proof checks the same selection property. `conditional-method.ts` and
`~/vl/vibelang-bugs/lemmascript-conditional-method/` reproduce a loop-backed call hoisted before its ternary union
guard. The production receiver uses the equivalent guarded statement, preserving behavior and every contract.
Neither reproduction is counted as a rejected mutation.

## Sixth module batch: history rank and validated replay

`decide` now specifies the exact append rank (zero or one above the maximum matching epic rank) and the first
matching insertion target's rank. `decideWithinCapacity`, which the writer actually calls, exposes every original
`decide` guarantee below capacity. History role standing specifies each of the five values; append, start and note
specify their contributor minimum.

`replayEvent` retains every `apply` postcondition while admitting a validated snapshot already at the maximum safe
cursor: every validated event is then stale and the snapshot is unchanged. `replayHistory` is the sequencer's actual
replay loop. It proves a safe monotone cursor, no more advances than input events, preservation of `0 <= rank < seq`,
and exact final numbering when events are contiguous from the initial snapshot. Schema decoding establishes its
preconditions. These clauses do not prove full replay-state equivalence, snapshot identity uniqueness, epic membership,
or journal ordering. The writer retains direct `apply`, whose precondition follows from an accepted capacity decision.

Two failing-first behavioral regressions found seed intent replay accepting an event past `MAX_SAFE_INTEGER`.
It now uses the same capacity decision, preserving the final safe event and refusing subsequent intents. The complete
seed intent fold and refusal order still need contracts; malformed arbitrary starting snapshots are not claimed safe.
No dependency or compiler behavior changes.

The full positive gate passes all twelve modules: **5,588 isolated conversation batches plus 154 ordinary
obligations** in 336.462 seconds. All **451 current mutations** reject: 144 fresh history mutations in 383.912 seconds
and 307 byte-identical inherited inputs with matching manifests and actual Dafny failures. All current source hashes
still match after the roles and benchmark rebase. The inventory now contains **1,582 executable items**. Two new
seed regressions failed first; the focused history suite passes 26 tests. The nine history/seed benchmark workloads
pass, checking their results throughout. After the final test-only rebase, typecheck and **545 unit/integration tests**
pass. The unchanged worker and browser inputs pass **33 local worker tests** (four opt-in skips) and **192 browser
tests** (nine opt-in skips). All command exits and intermediate failures remain in the lane report.

## Seventh module batch: web state projections and presentation

The production update now calls exact six-field projections for splash/minimize, palette, tour start/end/failure/
completion, docs toggling and Latest. Each helper specifies `surface`, `palette`, `tour`, `tourError`, `docs` and
`following`, including unchanged fields. Its explicit field constructors prevent an undeclared host-model field
from overwriting a newer client projection. The host retains its no-op guards, commands and account-menu priority.
This is a proof of those six fields, not every field of the entire Foldkit Model or account/People update.

`viewDecision` specifies exact refusal and status labels, entry display flags, Enter/Shift-Enter and whitespace
submission choices, view-transition destinations, sandbox pane/action guards and stale-response rejection. Media
contracts distinguish rendered video, reduced-motion poster and underlying failure kind: a poster still reports a
video asset's failure. The actual view and update use these decisions. No UI copy, behavior or dependency changes.

Finite coordinate comparisons in native scroll, wheel and touch policies now use LemmaScript's `real` alias.
`nearBottom` retains its original integer-model signature. `real-rounding.ts` demonstrates why exact real arithmetic
cannot establish native floating-point subtraction: the exact-real proof answers false for a height of 100, viewport
50 and y just below 2, while native subtraction rounds to 48 and answers true. NaN/Infinity and safe epoch/intent/
request-counter applicability remain outside these clauses. No production workaround or weakened contract is used.

The integrated typecheck passes. The positive gate proves all thirteen modules in 250.014 seconds (run-l_o1pui_,
conversation's 5,812 isolated assertion batches included), with fourteen harness, four fetch and five inventory
regressions green. The negative gate rejects all **564 executable mutations** — 113 new for this batch, including the
incoming `full` refusal consolidated into `viewDecision` — in 2,286.430 seconds (run-259piby3), every one an actual
Dafny rejection. The full unit suite passes **554 tests** in 75 files, the workerd suite passes 33 (four opt-in
skips), and the browser suite passes **192** (nine opt-in skips), all on the merged tree.

## Eighth module batch: budget charge keys

A review between batches found that the API forwarded the bare reply id to the day's shared budget object. Every
conversation numbers its first reply `r1`, so two conversations' first replies collided under one `reply/r1` charge
key: the second conversation's spend read as a replay of the first and added nothing. The workerd regression runs
two conversations through the real reply flow against the real budget object and reads the ledger's charge keys
through the test host's new window: one merged key before the fix, two namespaced keys after. `chargeKeyOf` in
`budgetDecision` is proved to construct exactly `host + ":" + reply`; the API and the test host both forward
through it. The uniqueness theorem behind it is string concat-cancellation, which the backend's string reasoning
cannot prove even with length hints — recorded as `verify/bugs/string-concat-unique.ts` with its host reproduction,
not weakened. Three executable mutations (host dropped, separator dropped, arguments swapped) each fail the exact
construction contract.

## Ninth module batch: exact sandbox guards and frames

Sandbox contracts now state exact admission and refusal precedence, first-match lookup (including malformed lists
with repeated conversations), exact replacement and append positions, complete metered-refusal frames and operation
identities. They retain every previously landed clause. Quantified source positions express these properties without
calling a loop-backed Dafny method from a postcondition (`method-spec.ts`). No runtime code changes from main.

The inherited unlanded attempt replaced runtime loops with recursive helpers. Two new regressions on 20,000-entry
ledgers fail with `RangeError` on that candidate. Restoring the existing iterative runtime makes both pass and
retains the stronger properties, which freshly verify. The specification-only recursive running-count measure gains
an explicit checked induction step; the harness checks its signature and fresh empty body before insertion, just
as for the conversation pending-count lemma. This is not a native stack-safety theorem.

The newer walkthrough added another matching tour guard, so the EndTour mutation now anchors its named case.
The selected receiver-pane clause follows the current person-or-streaming-agent policy. Generator ambiguity and
missing selected clauses fail closed; parser failures and timeouts never count as rejections. The full positive gate passes thirteen modules: 19,863 isolated conversation batches plus 184 ordinary units in
its other twelve modules. The current **652/652** mutant copies match actual intended-function Dafny rejections:
650 from the full 651-case invocation, the repaired receiver omission and one first-match-specific control.
The full invocation's receiver timeout and its unsuccessful retries are excluded. Narrowing that injected omission
to a concrete first post (`Posted #1`, empty conversation) reaches the same unchanged count contract in 15.236 s.
The first-match control returns a later adjacent match, retaining the earlier index-validity guarantees while
violating the new first-match clause. Regenerating all copies and manifest entries checks byte/hash equality with
these receipts. The limits remain 30 seconds per negative assertion and 60 per positive assertion.

After rebasing on `635f4dd3`, app/Worker typechecks and 810 unit/integration tests pass (one skipped). Before that
host/client rebase, 52 local workerd and 264 browser cases pass (three/twelve opt-in skips respectively).
All thirteen proof-module source hashes remain identical through that rebase. The inventory contains 2,458 items.
Exact command exits, timings, paths and failed runs are in the host lane report.

The browser and history page call `startupDecision.vibe` for explicit-query precedence, preference writes,
authoritative role-list membership/defaults and the mode switch. Tutorial startup preserves saved sessions (including

The six new production obligations pass; all nineteen new mutants reject. All fourteen current proof-module
hashes and all 671 current source/manifest inputs match the positive/rejection receipts. App/Worker typechecks,

## Eleventh module batch: theatre presentation decisions

The injected browser presentation service calls the exact proved target selector, speech action, saved audio identity,
telemetry outcome and enabled-control focus index. The speech state machine cancels muted/ended playback, pauses or
cancels a paused seek, resumes a retained utterance, speaks each new identity once and keeps an unchanged redraw
silent. Native storage/JSON, speech API execution and layout geometry remain boundaries. No UI words change.

The five production functions pass ten proof units and all 43 new mutants reject. All fifteen current marked
source hashes and 714 current mutant copies match actual positive/rejection receipts. App/Worker typechecks,
826 unit/integration cases and thirty affected browser cases pass (one unit opt-in skipped).

The extraction audit found that saved audio JSON was used without a shape check: `null` broke sound control, and
moving short-circuit choices into a helper also exposed eager reads for muted control. A faulty injected Window
service reproduces both failures, then a stronger regression catches a wrong Boolean shape. The host now admits
only a bounded integer step and Boolean sound/active/paused fields before calling the kernel. The existing fallback
handles malformed JSON and unavailable storage. Sixteen storage cases plus the five existing walkthrough units
pass after the fix. Browser speech observers now delegate to real native methods instead of no-op replacements;
these are dispatch/control observations, not a claim of audible headless speech. Exact exits and receipts are in
the host report.

## Twelfth module batch: live dispatch scan

The live reply dispatcher now runs `liveDispatchDecision` through scan, attempt and done. Each observed mark skips
exactly one source position; the first unmarked position becomes the only attempt, and both engine success and busy
refusal finish this invocation. A successful start alone writes its mark. Exhaustion reads no extra storage key.
This preserves original storage, intent, start, log and mark ordering and calls the shared host on both workerd and
Alchemy. Engine/lock/journal correctness and stored-mark truth remain host/library assumptions.

All five production proof units and thirteen new mutants pass their gates. All sixteen current source hashes
and 727 current mutant copies/manifest entries match actual proof and intended-function rejection receipts.
App/Worker typechecks, 826 unit/integration cases and 52 local workerd cases pass (one/three opt-in skips).
The inventory contains 2,469 items. Exact command exits and receipt scope are in the host report.

## Thirteenth module batch: Post and StartTour continuations

The actual Post and StartTour bodies now thread begun/recorded/refused/dispatched state through their existing
journaled record and dispatch Actions. The shared kernel proves the reachable invariant: records are zero or one,
dispatches are zero or one and never exceed records, and a dispatched run retains its accepted null receipt. The
first record/refusal is immutable; dispatched/refused runs cannot dispatch again. Existing dispatch admission,
append, tour-end and reading-time contracts remain verbatim, and the old admission helper is reused by the model.

The updated flow module passes thirteen proof units (five new); all 43 of its mutants reject (28 new). All sixteen
current marked source hashes and 755 current mutant copies/manifest entries match actual positive/rejection
receipts. App/Worker typechecks, 826 unit/integration cases and 52 local workerd cases pass (one/three opt-in skips).
The inventory contains 2,474 executable items.

Step names, public answers, Effect ordering and output-only metrics/logs remain unchanged. This is the modeled
body order; engine journal/replay idempotency and interleavings remain library/host assumptions. Exact exits and
receipt scope are in the host report.

## Fourteenth module batch: accepted issue ancestry

`canLinkIssues` now carries a bounded ancestor-path witness. The independently specified recursive measure
`issueAncestorAt` describes exactly one first-match parent link at each step, an absorbing root and the missing-node
sentinel. The unchanged iterative runtime proves that every accepted path reaches root zero within at most
`items.length + 1` links and excludes the child at every earlier position. Existing positivity, distinctness and
child-existence contracts remain verbatim. This establishes safety of the selected parent path; complete graph
preservation and acceptance completeness are not claimed.

The full production conversation proof passes 19,896 isolated batches in 724.340 seconds. Fifteen new executable
mutations reject through actual Dafny contract failures. Removing the redundant early self-check is equivalent to
the existing loop's child check, so that survivor is excluded and replaced by erroneous self-link acceptance.
The 233 current conversation mutation inputs all match actual rejections across the full run and replacement
receipt. The earlier full command exits one solely for its obsolete equivalent self-guard deletion; that case is
excluded. All sixteen current marked modules and 770 current mutants match successful proof/rejection hashes.
App/Worker typechecks and 827 unit/integration cases in 123 files pass after rebasing onto the incoming
HTTP-client-test commit 6db1ba51 (one opt-in skip; Vitest 226.09 seconds). The inventory contains 2,476 executable items.

## Fifteenth module batch: Reply accounting and completion

The actual Reply event fold calls a proved initial/reset/append/charge/invalid/failure kernel. Zero initial usage
and each exact nonnegative addition establish the sum recurrence for admitted settled calls, with every intermediate
sum at most MAX_SAFE_INTEGER. The admission decision requires all three native safe-integer observations and the
exact sum domain; no invalid settlement subtracts or poisons previously valid accounting. Invalidity latches through
all retries, text, failures and later valid settlements. Completion can publish an answer only when the agent succeeds,
accounting remains valid and text is nonempty; failed/empty precedence is explicit.

The prior fold accepts negative, fractional, NaN, infinite and unsafe usage. Seven failing-first numeric cases show
lost or poisoned earlier spend (for example, 120 followed by -140 becomes -20); an eighth checks the valid exact
boundary and the new integrity flag. The fix retains known valid spend, rejects malformed settlements and returns
the existing generic failure rather than a successful reply with invalid accounting. The outcome-only log records
`invalid-usage`, never provider messages or input values. Step names and durable operation order stay unchanged.

The new module has nine functions and ten proof units; 47 executable mutants reject. Current source/manifest hashes
match seventeen positive modules and 817 actual rejected mutations. Twenty-two targeted reply tests, all nine reply
fuzz targets (139,674 executions, one retained regression replay) and both affected agent benchmarks pass. The fuzz
oracle uses independent unbounded BigInt arithmetic inside Effect. Final app/Worker typechecks, 835 unit/integration
cases (one opt-in skip; 124 files, 233.95 seconds) and 52 local workerd cases (three opt-in skips; 93.72 seconds) pass.
No new dependency is added. The inventory contains 2,485 executable items.

The wrapper still owns event-tag/payload projection, DoneText parsing and native safe-integer observations. These
contracts do not claim full event-routing or SDK replay correctness. Reply invokes the AgentAction implementation
rather than a cached outer Action, while sealed internal model-step replay re-emits recorded events; this is an SDK
assumption inspected in its implementation and EventSink contract, not a new theorem.

## Sixteenth module batch: shared history-page decisions

Twelve additional decisions live beside the history flow machine's existing authoritative role ranking. Actual
role checks, history HTTP writers/readers, Plan flows and browser controls call them: exact role threshold, response
classification, refusal status, agent signature, append-versus-retell permission, live-mode role reset, open-epic
membership and opening prefix, Plan operation validation, initial epic and new/existing epic fields, badge and refusal
wording. Existing Effect order, commands, model fields and UI copy are preserved. The only role order is the existing
`standing`; no second ranking is introduced.

The updated module passes 23 proof units (twelve added functions) and all 61 current mutations reject (45 new).
All seventeen current production module hashes and 862 current mutated sources/manifests match actual proof and
rejection receipts. App/Worker typechecks pass; the full unit suite passes 835 cases/124 files (one opt-in skip,
208.56 seconds), local workerd passes 52 cases (three opt-in skips, 71.49 seconds), and /history browser tests pass all 21
cases on 4504 (46.7 seconds). The inventory has 2,497 executable items. Complete history page merges, asynchronous result admission and model frames remain work;
this is not a claim that the entire history UI is proved. The unchanged tool request projection remains named by the
optional-union-default reproduction.

## Seventeenth module batch: complete Reply event routing

The native SDK wrapper now projects only payload fields, DoneText and integer observations. The pure reducer owns
every progress choice: requested/retried reset, text-only append, admitted settlement or invalidity latch, failure code,
and identity for every other event. From a zero initial state, the reducer closes the nonnegative safe token invariant
and never clears invalid accounting. Each event clause specifies the complete relevant field frame; ignored and
non-text events return the prior state.

The updated production module passes thirteen proof units (ten functions), and all 61 current module mutants reject
(fourteen new). Seventeen current module hashes and 876 current mutated sources/manifests match actual receipts.
Final app/Worker typechecks pass; 835 unit/integration cases in 124 files pass (one opt-in skip, 201.05 seconds);
local workerd passes 52 cases (three opt-in skips, 70.46 seconds); all 9 reply fuzz targets pass 119,738 executions with
one retained regression replay (49.71 seconds), and both affected benchmarks pass (6.71 seconds). The inventory
contains 2,498 executable items. Native SDK payload decoding/projection, DoneText, intrinsic observations and effect/journal
execution remain boundaries; the policy dispatch itself is now proved.

The direct conditional postcondition over an optional record member exposes the same bad Option emission even
without a union output. `optional-member-default.ts` is retained at
`~/vl/vibelang-bugs/lemmascript-optional-member-default/` (strict source, two resolution/type errors). The reducer's
exact text relation is stated as both the undefined and present implications, preserving both branches and every
field. Runtime code and domain are unchanged; no contract case is dropped.

## Eighteenth module batch: Reply body order

The actual Reply body threads a verified run through its completed start receipt, composition entry/completion and
settlement receipt. Only `done` admits composition; ready follows only a completed composition; settlement is
admitted only after ready. Each receipt/stage is zero or one, with settlements <= compositions <= start receipts.
Refused and terminal runs admit no later work. All transitions preserve the exact reachable invariant and unrelated
fields. The zero completed-start count represents no observed receipt; once one arrives its nonnullable raw string is
retained. Retry attempts are not counted as separate start receipts.

Action names, input/output, retry policy, interruption propagation and Effect order stay unchanged. A counts-only
`vibelang.reply.order` log observes the completed stages. The real engine/logger test fails in both budget and
configuration cases before instrumentation, then passes with exactly one record even after replay and with no post
text/person name in serialized annotations; the unknown-post refusal still produces no answer.

The flow module passes 21 proof units (eight added functions), and all 102 current module mutants reject (59 new).
Seventeen current source hashes and 935 current mutations match actual proof/rejection receipts. Typechecks pass,
the full suite passes 835 cases/124 files (one opt-in skip, 201.92 seconds), local workerd passes 52 cases (three opt-in
skips, 70.91 seconds), and the targeted three real-engine cases pass. The inventory has 2,506 executable items.
This is per-invocation body order; physical retry attempts, SDK journaling/replay and external service execution
remain explicit assumptions. Conversation contracts independently enforce at most one answer per post.

`nullable-member-inequality.ts` / `~/vl/vibelang-bugs/lemmascript-nullable-member-inequality/` retains a strict
nullable-member non-null/not-done predicate in body and contract. LemmaScript emits an Option<string>/string
comparison in both positions (two resolution errors). No successful proof is claimed for that fixture and all older
production contracts remain verbatim.

## Nineteenth module batch: Tour body and accepted end ordering

EndTour now uses the same verified record-before-follow-up model with its own `end` kind: only an accepted end
admits one wake, and refused/terminal continuations are immutable. The Tour body observes current activity before
entering ready, consumes ready to start one speaker and records the winning finished/ended outcome. Inactive,
finished and ended states admit no later speaker. Every transition preserves the exact phase/outcome/count
invariant, with the speaker count between zero and one. Actual Action names, race, cancellation and outcome-only
metrics/logs are unchanged; conversation contracts independently reject all text after the tour ends.

The flow module passes 27 proof units (six added functions); all 129 current module mutants reject (27 new, including
the end kind). Seventeen current source hashes and 962 current mutation inputs match actual receipts. App/Worker
typechecks pass after rebase onto 1b5decf5; 838 unit cases/127 files pass (one opt-in skip, 216.30 seconds), 52 local workerd cases pass (three opt-in
skips, 70.85 seconds), 15 targeted tour/walkthrough cases pass, and all 33 tour/repair/walkthrough browser cases pass again on 4504
(3.3 minutes). After the incoming agent-placeholder cleanup the inventory has 2,508 executable items; all 17 source/962 mutation receipt identities still match. SDK scheduling, physical retries, cancellation and eventual
completion remain host assumptions; the complete walk control loop is subsequent work.

The prospective full cursor model exposes two narrower tool gaps: `nullable-record-guard.ts` emits a member on an
Option despite its non-null guard, and `nullable-record-union.ts` emits incompatible nested null/record variants.
Both strictly typecheck and fail backend resolution, with exact contracts retained under
`~/vl/vibelang-bugs/lemmascript-nullable-record-guard/` and
`~/vl/vibelang-bugs/lemmascript-nullable-record-union/`. Neither is counted as a proof or mutation rejection.

## Product defects

**Saved theatre audio shape.** An injected Window service returning JSON `null` breaks speech control; wrong-type
Boolean fields can incorrectly suppress a new utterance. Validate StoredAudio before its fields reach the proved
policy. Three strengthened regressions fail before validation, and all sixteen pass afterward.

- **Two conversations shared one budget charge.** The daily ledger dedupes by reply id, and every conversation's
  first reply is `r1`; the API forwarded it bare, so a second conversation's first reply was never charged. Found
  between batches by reviewing the wiring the proofs call; the regression failed before the namespaced key fix.

- **Seed replay could overflow a valid cursor.** Starting at the safe maximum, or reaching it on one intent and then
  replaying another, produced cursor `9007199254740992`. Both regressions failed before the capacity decision was
  applied to seed replay.

- **Conversation storage bypassed its numeric and event-shape proof domain.** A faulty Log could supply negative,
  fractional or unsafe cursors, malformed text or unknown variants before replay. EventSchema decoding now rejects
  these rows with no write/dispatch and no stored content in diagnostics. The regression failed before the fix.


- **Unsafe history ranks broke distinct insertion positions.** Plain numeric schemas admitted negative, fractional,
  nonfinite and unsafe integer ranks. Safe integer plus `rank < seq` validation now protects every replay/bootstrap
  boundary, and the proved writer refuses capacity exhaustion. The final safe event is stored once; the next intent
  is refused without writes. Four regression tests failed before the fix; all six boundary tests now pass.
- **Cached history follow bypassed validation; snapshot errors exposed stored content.** A later store read could
  stream an invalid event, and HistoryObject's early JSON decode could reveal stored text in diagnostics. Follow
  validates each reread; stored JSON uses a redacted decoder. Both new regressions failed before the fixes.

- **Unsafe usage and corrupt totals bypassed accounting's proof domain.** Overflow could replace a valid daily sum
  with an inexact value; a negative total could be charged or admitted. Admission and spend now check persisted
  safe integers and a proved sum guard. Overflow and invalid totals fail without writes; exact maximum charging and
  repeated oversized telemetry are covered by regressions.

- **Non-finite usage poisoned the daily ledger.** A provider's NaN or Infinity made the total unusable. The regression
  `budgetProof.test.vibe` failed first with NaN instead of 13. Non-finite usage now adds zero; finite negative usage
  still clamps to zero and finite usage retains its rounding. Existing spends, the storage lock, logging and metrics remain.

- **The proof harness could check zero sources.** A prospective source nested under `.checks` was ignored by the
  directory scanner, so it said “0 files checked” and exited zero. `verify/harness_test.py` reproduces that failure
  first. The harness now supplies every staged source explicitly, rejects an empty selection, and runs its regression
  tests before every proof invocation. Production and mutation receipts preceding this fix checked 4 and 84 sources;
  the prospective receipts with zero checked sources are superseded, not counted as validation.

- **Lifecycle tests assumed Web Crypto had finished after 200 yields.** The baseline flow reproduced the failure.
  The concurrency and disposal tests now await real ReplyFinished/ReplyDelta events and retain their assertions,
  including live follower and worker shutdown. The repaired lifecycle suite passes 9/9 and the full suite 177/177.
- **Discovery and inventory could omit source.** Whitespace-aware marker discovery now has a regression. The
  inventory used UTF-16 positions against byte-based projection mappings; it now reads original-source ESTree,
  includes methods/effect blocks, rejects parse failures, and passes four source-enumeration regressions.

- **The startup browser check assumed health always beat its deadline.** The faulty transport now observes
  cancellation. Review rejected accepting either timeout or settlement, which could hide blocked keyboard handling.
  The browser host injects a Clock that holds the health deadline, while forwarding real time reads and other sleeps;
  startup passes its Clock service to the backend resource layer. Keyboard/post assertions deliberately run beyond
  the ordinary deadline and require health to remain uncancelled, then settle on release. The regression failed first;
  all 12 startup browser cases pass. Independent backend TestClock cases still require the exact three-second timeout.
  The 6,737-commit bootstrap integration exceeded the default five-second unit budget under shared host load; its
  integration budget is 20 seconds, with all replay equality, uniqueness and count assertions unchanged.

- **The announcement browser test could miss all partial renders.** Under shared host load, rendering coalesced the
  real deltas and terminal event before the browser observed a nonempty unfinished answer. The transport fault now
  holds each real ReplyFinished until the test observes the nonempty streaming text and unchanged announcement,
  then releases it. It preserves all event contents, order, accessibility checks and complete-answer assertions.

- **The sandbox alarm test assumed a fixed number of scheduler yields completed native asynchronous work.**
  The inherited test failed with no later meter event after startup. Its logger now signals an observer installed
  only after startup completes; the test awaits a real later alarm event and retains the idle/count-increase/stopped
  assertions. Review caught that an earlier observer could be satisfied by startup's own meter log; that preliminary
  receipt is superseded. The corrected focused test passes.

## Tool gaps

- `~/vl/vibelang-bugs/lemmascript-inline-record-parameter/`: LemmaScript 0.6.6 emits an invalid Dafny type name for an
  inline record parameter. The isolated reproduction and failing receipt are retained. The failure decision is now a
  code classifier; Reply retains the static product-wording lookup.
- LemmaScript 0.6.6 omits object-valued module constants (`REASONS`), producing unresolved identifiers. The failing reproduction is `verify/bugs/object-constant.ts` and
  `~/vl/vibelang-bugs/lemmascript-object-constant/`. No proof of the object lookup is claimed.
- Existing backend reproductions remain in `verify/bugs/` (0005). No contract is weakened to pass them.

- `~/vl/vibelang-bugs/lemmascript-string-slice-clamp/`: `post.slice(1)` is incorrectly lowered to a
  potentially out-of-bounds Dafny slice. Its total contract is preserved in the reproduction. `replyIdOf` remains
  unproved; no nonempty-string precondition was added to make it pass.

The new gap probes all strictly typecheck and fail as tool errors (0/9 files proved including the four existing
fixtures), retained in `~/vl/vl-proveall.gaps.log`:

| Reproduction in `verify/bugs/` | Failure | Unproved app boundary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `finite-round.ts` | Unsupported `Number.isFinite`; `Math.round` has no lowering | Provider usage normalization and IEEE-754 inputs |
| `json-codec.ts` | Unsupported `JSON.stringify` | Schema/JSON encode-decode round trips and SSE payloads |
| `uri-codec.ts` | Unresolved `encodeURIComponent` and `decodeURIComponent` | URI encoding, malformed escape handling and lone surrogates |
| `regexp.ts` | Unsupported regular expression | Docs URL allowlist and Markdown fence/inline parsing |
| `object-constant.ts` | Unresolved object constant | Static reason wording lookup |

Two additional probes strictly typecheck but do not verify:

- `unicode-case.ts` / `~/vl/vibelang-bugs/lemmascript-unicode-case/`: JavaScript maps `K` to `k`, while
  Dafny's emitted StringToLower only maps ASCII A–Z. The correct contract fails. Unicode normalization in keyboard
  shortcuts and mock selection remains a runtime boundary; normalized-input policies can still be proved.
- `event-key.ts` / `~/vl/vibelang-bugs/lemmascript-event-key/`: production `String(seq).padStart(16, "0")`
  fails emission. The fixed-width key contract is retained; storage-key lexical ordering is not proved.

- `filter-method.ts` / `~/vl/vibelang-bugs/lemmascript-filter-method/`: a single-expression Array.filter callback
  calling a verified loop-backed predicate fails as an unsupported multi-statement Dafny lambda. The unanswered-post
  selector's complete production contract is retained in its prospective proof; no selection proof is yet claimed.

- `empty-string-union.ts` / `~/vl/vibelang-bugs/lemmascript-empty-string-union/`: an empty-string alternative in a
  string-literal union becomes an empty Dafny constructor (`Route.`), which the backend cannot parse. The typed route
  reproduction retains both exact outcomes; no production contract is suppressed.

- `union-widening.ts` / `~/vl/vibelang-bugs/lemmascript-union-widening/`: a narrow string union returned as its
  wider union strictly typechecks, but the backend emits incompatible Dafny datatypes. The pending overlay-priority
  extraction exposed this gap. Its exact-value reproduction is retained; the existing UI remains intact, with no
  production workaround or weakened contract.

- `real-rounding.ts` / `~/vl/vibelang-bugs/lemmascript-real-rounding/`: an exact-real arithmetic theorem passes,
  while native execution of the same function violates it at `(100, 50, 1.9999999999999998)`. The native assertion
  intentionally fails. This records the IEEE-754 boundary; neither result is counted as a rejected mutation.

Each new fixture also lives at `~/vl/vibelang-bugs/lemmascript-<name>/` with its command. These are not mutation
successes. Compiler/runtime execution of Effect, Schema and Foldkit remains outside LemmaScript's plain subset.

The resumed codec/Markdown probes retain `json-roundtrip.ts` (all message fields), `uri-roundtrip.ts` (printable
ASCII, excluding native malformed-surrogate exceptions), and the exact production `markdown-safe-link.ts` scheme
filter. All strictly typecheck; JSON.stringify and regex literals fail emission, and URI codecs are unresolved in
Dafny. These refine the existing codec/regexp gaps; none is counted as a proof or rejected mutation. Reproductions
are `~/vl/vibelang-bugs/lemmascript-<fixture-name>/`.

The history tool request prototype additionally exposes `optional-union-default.ts`, reproduced at
`~/vl/vibelang-bugs/lemmascript-optional-union-default/`: a strictly typed two-variant output with a defaulted
optional input emits incompatible bare-string/Option branches and a match on `seq<char>`. Dafny reports two
resolution/type errors, not a contract failure. The production `requestOf` remains unchanged and unproved; the
exact reproduction contract is retained.

`safe-usage-integer.ts` / `~/vl/vibelang-bugs/lemmascript-safe-usage-integer/` strictly typechecks, but native
`Number.isSafeInteger` fails Dafny emission as an unsupported method call. The exact range contract is retained.
The Reply integer kernel proves its admission policy over the supplied native observations; malformed-number
regressions exercise the actual observation calls. This is a tool failure and never a rejected mutation.

## Inventory

The compiler's original-source ESTree identifies executable functions, methods, effect blocks and nested callbacks
without counting generated wrappers or code examples inside strings. `node verify/inventory.cjs --write` refreshes this table; without `--write` it rejects drift. Pure
functions with unsupported library operations remain unproved until a documented reproduction or upstream fix exists.

Tourfix (0014) adds the exact reading-hold policy and its three executable mutations. The combined positive gate
passes 101 obligations across eight modules; all three new mutations fail their production contracts. Word
tokenization, rendering and durable execution retain the boundaries stated above.

### Twentieth batch: walkthrough counters and scalar controls

The actual walk uses `nextTourCounter` for private visit/hold identities. Its native-number predecessor fails the
regression after `Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER` (unreachable in practice: about 9 quadrillion steps in one tour): the next two values both become 9007199254740992, repeating durable
step names. Private counters now use BigInt, with an exact-plus-one contract, strict progress and nonnegative closure.
They never enter journal payloads; step names remain decimal strings and match every previously exact counter value.
`firstTourVisit` proves zero-only saved-state admission; `tourWait` proves pause waits for control without starting a
reading clock; `tourControlChoice` proves exact seek versus retained current step; `nextTourStep` proves one bounded
clock advance. The actual clock cursor is bounded by the finite walkthrough script. Existing Actions, log/span/count
steps and cancellation are retained. No new dependency, UI copy or compiler behavior changes.

The seven counter regressions and 33 flow-module proof units pass. All 141 current module mutants (12 new) reject, and
the full proof gate passes 17/17 modules. The full optional restored-cursor composition remains unproved because the exact minimal
nullable-record guard/union contracts still fail backend resolution; those contracts are retained unchanged. BigInt
itself compiles and verifies successfully and is not a tool gap. Whole-app proof coverage remains incomplete.

### Twenty-first batch: web update guards

`src/web/modelDecision.vibe` holds 19 decisions that `update` in `src/web/main.vibe` previously wrote inline; every
call site now runs the proved function, with no behavior change. Stale-response fences cover actions epochs (an absent
epoch reads as zero), issue retries and details, and wiki requests. `slashHighlight` stays inside the menu after the
menu shrinks. 23 proof units; 49 new mutations reject. `update` itself remains unproved: LemmaScript cannot read the
Foldkit message match or the Model spreads, so the frame of each arm is covered by tests.
